<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算能否为相对论束缚态问题（hBSE）带来实际优势。hBSE的离散化可自然映射为矩阵本征值问题，理论上适合量子本征求解器；因此作者希望系统检验这一映射的量子可解性与潜在优势边界。

Method: 对两个大质量相对论标量粒子经梯近似标量交换的束缚态hBSE，先做Wick旋转至欧氏空间并作O(4) S波分波投影，将hBSE约化为维度N=2^n的对称矩阵本征值问题；再把哈密顿量分解为n量子比特Pauli算符之和，采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）张量网络拟设的变分量子本征求解器（VQE）求解，并与经典对角化对比，同时分析纠缠结构与三重障碍（Pauli开销、纠缠标度、梯度尺度/贫瘠高原）。

Result: 在N=16（n=4量子比特）时，VQE对最大本征值（对应最小束缚耦合常数）的平均相对误差优于1%。BSE振幅的纠缠熵在所有测试尺度上表现出低且近似满足面积律的纠缠，支持MPS拟设的有效性。三重障碍分析显示Pauli项指数增长、纠缠近尺度无关地偏低、以及拟设梯度随规模下降（与贫瘠高原趋势一致），说明所研究问题落在经典可解范畴，可由低键维张量网络及MPS/Lanczos高效处理。

Conclusion: 该负面结果首次给出量子比特编码下BSE振幅的纠缠量化，建立了适用于未来BSE变体的Pauli哈密顿量框架，并指出二维Minkowski空间BSE、N体束缚态及非梯近似核等物理动机明确的扩展方向，这些方向可能更可能出现真正的量子优势。

Abstract: We present a gate-based quantum computing solution of the homogeneous Bethe-Salpeter equation (hBSE) for the bound state of two massive relativistic scalar particles interacting via ladder-approximation scalar exchange. After Wick rotation to Euclidean space and O(4) S-wave partial-wave projection, the hBSE is reduced to a symmetric matrix eigenvalue problem of dimension N = 2^n. We decompose the resulting Hamiltonian into a sum of n-qubit Pauli operators and solve it with the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) using a Matrix Product State (MPS) tensor-network ansatz. For N=16 (n=4 qubits), the VQE recovers the maximum eigenvalue - which encodes the minimum coupling constant for binding - to better than 1 % mean relative error compared to classical diagonalization. Entanglement analysis of the BSE amplitude shows low, area-law-like entanglement over the tested sizes, which motivates the MPS ansatz. A critical analysis of three independent barriers - exponential Pauli overhead, approximately size-independent low entanglement, and decreasing VQE gradient scales for the tested ansatz, consistent with generic barren-plateau concerns at larger n - reveals that the specific problem studied here lies in a classically tractable regime: over the tested range it is well described by low-bond-dimension tensor networks and efficiently handled by MPS/Lanczos methods. This negative result provides, to our knowledge, the first entanglement quantification of the BSE amplitude in qubit encoding, establishes the Pauli Hamiltonian framework for future BSE variants, and identifies 2D Minkowski-space BSE, N-body bound states, and non-ladder kernels as physically motivated extensions where genuine quantum advantage may become plausible.

</details>


### [94] [New lower bounds for CDS and $f$-routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24291)
*Atsuya Hasegawa,Ranitha Mataraarachchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非局域量子计算（NLQC）中 f-routing 的纠缠代价下界问题，通过建立 robust CDS 的共享随机性下界与 one-sided-perfect f-routing 的纠缠代价下界，推进了完全鲁棒设定下路由问题的下界研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 NLQC 的纠缠代价与复杂性理论、密码学、量子引力等领域相关。$f$-routing 是核心特例，其动机部分来自量子位置验证，而在完全鲁棒设定下证明其纠缠代价下界是该领域的主要开放问题。本文受此问题驱动，寻求相关下界。

Method: 1) 研究鲁棒条件秘密披露（CDS）的共享随机性代价，利用 Allerstorfer 等（Quantum 2024）建立的 CDS 与 $f$-routing 之间的联系；2) 考虑 one-sided-perfect $f$-routing（在一个输入类上精确、另一类上常数误差），利用 Asadi, Culf, May（ITCS 2025）方法中出现的低秩矩阵的正性，推导基于符号秩的纠缠代价下界。

Result: 鲁棒 CDS 的共享随机性代价下界为确定性 SMP 通信复杂度的对数，即使通信与私有随机性不受限制；该下界对相等函数（equality function）是紧的。对于 one-sided-perfect $f$-routing，得到以符号秩表示的纠缠代价一般下界；特别地，在内积函数（inner-product function）的两种 one-sided-perfect 设定下，得到线性纠缠代价下界，与已知上界匹配。

Conclusion: 本文通过 robust CDS 随机性复杂度与符号秩方法，分别给出了与 $f$-routing 纠缠代价相关的两个下界，推进了完全鲁棒路由下界问题，并在内积函数情形达到紧确结果。

Abstract: Understanding the entanglement cost of non-local quantum computation (NLQC) is relevant to complexity theory, cryptography, quantum gravity, and related areas. A central special case is $f$-routing, motivated in part by quantum position verification. Proving lower bounds on its entanglement cost in the fully robust setting has been a major open problem in NLQC.
  Motivated by this problem, we establish two related lower bounds. First, we study the shared-randomness cost of robust conditional disclosure of secrets (CDS). The connection between CDS and $f$-routing established by Allerstorfer et al. (Quantum 2024) makes understanding the randomness complexity of robust CDS a natural step toward lower bounds for the fully robust routing problem. We show that the shared-randomness cost of robust CDS is lower bounded by the logarithm of deterministic SMP communication complexity, even when communication and private randomness are unrestricted. Our lower bound is tight for the equality function.
  Second, we consider one-sided-perfect $f$-routing, in which the protocol is exact on one input class and has constant error on the other. By exploiting the positivity of the low-rank matrix arising in the method of Asadi, Culf, and May (ITCS 2025), we derive a general lower bound on the entanglement cost in terms of sign rank. In particular, this yields a linear lower bound on the entanglement cost of routing for the inner-product function in both one-sided-perfect settings, matching the known upper bound.

</details>


### [95] [A Sharp Noise Threshold for Shor's Quantum Factoring and Discrete Log Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24316)
*Jin-Yi Cai,Ben Young*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究噪声影响量子傅里叶变换中受控旋转门时，Shor量子因数分解与离散对数算法的渐近行为，给出了一个尖锐且极小的噪声阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Cai (2024)以及Cai和Young (2025)的结果，更精确地刻画Shor算法在受控旋转门噪声下的鲁棒性边界。

Method: 对Shor量子因数分解和离散对数算法中量子傅里叶变换的精确受控旋转门引入噪声，进行渐近分析，并确定其噪声阈值。

Result: 识别出一个尖锐且消失般微小的噪声阈值：噪声水平低于该阈值时，两种算法能在期望多项式时间内成功；高于该阈值时，当底层素数属于一个正密度集合时，算法可证明无法在期望多项式时间内求解相应问题。

Conclusion: Shor算法在此噪声模型下存在精确的相变阈值，阈值为尖锐且极小，刻画了算法成功与失败之间的分界。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of Shor's quantum factoring and discrete log algorithms when noise affects the precise controlled rotation gates in their quantum Fourier transforms. Improving the results of Cai (2024) and Cai and Young (2025), we identify a sharp and vanishingly small noise threshold. If the noise level lies below this threshold, then the two algorithms succeed in expected polynomial time. If the noise level exceeds this threshold, then the algorithms provably fail to solve their respective problems in expected polynomial time when the underlying primes belong to a set of positive density.

</details>


### [96] [Robust self-testing of nonmaximal entanglement from a reduced inner product game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24349)
*Ranyiliu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将Lalonde伪传态内积博弈从六维约化到五维，证明该约化博弈能自检验其非最大纠缠态及全部测量效应，并给出鲁棒性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约化Lalonde伪传态内积博弈的维度（从六维到五维），同时保持Alice四问、Bob三问的结构并将答案字母表缩至五个，以获得更简洁且可自检验的博弈。

Method: 通过维度约化构造一个新博弈（保留原四/三问结构，答案各为五元），随后证明其自检验性质，并用局部提取信道下的最优平方保真度Ξ定义距离度量d_ext，结合紧性论证给出定性鲁棒性。

Result: 证明约化博弈可自检验其非最大纠缠态与全部35个测量效应；鲁棒性为：获胜概率≥1-ε的策略满足 d_ext ≤ min{1, 2^21 √ε}，其中 d_ext=√(1-Ξ)。

Conclusion: 该自检验结果意味着任何有限维最大纠缠态均无法以概率1完美赢得该博弈。

Abstract: We reduce Lalonde's pseudo-telepathy inner product game from six to five dimensions, keeping its four Alice questions and three Bob questions and reducing each answer alphabet to five. We prove that the reduced game self-tests its nonmaximally entangled state and all 35 measurement effects. The self-test is also robust: for a strategy winning with probability at least $1-\varepsilon$ we obtain $d_{\mathrm{ext}}\le\min\{1,2^{21}\sqrt{\varepsilon}\}$, where $d_{\mathrm{ext}}=\sqrt{1-Ξ}$ and $Ξ$ is the optimal squared fidelity with the reference state under local extraction channels. A compactness argument then gives simultaneous qualitative robustness for all measurement actions on the state under common local isometries. As a consequence of the self-test, no maximally entangled state of any finite dimension can win the game perfectly.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum Work Extraction via Conditional Spatial Displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24425)
*Necati Çelik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种借助连续变量指针的测量辅助反馈协议，从相干量子电池中提取功，并在二能级电池与高斯指针模型中验证 daemonic ergotropy 等于标准 ergotropy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池与指针纠缠后，若追踪掉指针自由度，可提取功会变得不可及；作者希望借助测量辅助反馈恢复这部分功，并给出一个物理上直观的量子麦克斯韦妖实现。

Method: 采用酉算符 U=exp(-ikt H⊗P/ħ) 使电池能量本征态与辅助指针位置纠缠；随后对指针位置做投影测量，条件性制备电池纯态，再通过反馈酉操作提取功。针对二能级量子电池与高斯指针，计算条件态及对应 daemonic ergotropy。

Result: 对所考虑的纯初态，发现 daemonic ergotropy 在所有相互作用强度下均等于标准 ergotropy，即测量辅助反馈完全恢复了因与指针纠缠并追踪其自由度而原本不可及的功。

Conclusion: 该协议给出了量子麦克斯韦妖的透明物理实现，指针充当量子测量辅助体，其位置与电池能量相关联；方案可在囚禁离子系统实现，有助于理解量子相干性与测量在热力学中的作用。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for extracting work from a coherent quantum battery state by exploiting measurement-assisted feedback mediated by a continuous-variable pointer. The scheme relies on the unitary operator $U = \exp(-i k t \, \hat{H} \otimes \hat{P}/\hbar)$, which generates entanglement between the battery's energy eigenstates and the position of an auxiliary pointer. A subsequent projective measurement of the pointer's position conditionally prepares the battery in a pure state from which work can be extracted via a feedback unitary. We analyze the protocol for a two-level quantum battery and a Gaussian pointer, computing the conditional states and the corresponding daemonic ergotropy. For the pure initial state considered, we find that the daemonic ergotropy equals the standard ergotropy for all interaction strengths, demonstrating that the measurement-assisted feedback recovers the full extractable work that would otherwise become inaccessible due to entanglement with the pointer when its degrees of freedom are traced out. The protocol thus provides a physically transparent realization of a quantum Maxwell demon, where the pointer acts as a quantum measurement ancilla whose position becomes correlated with the battery's energy. The scheme is amenable to experimental implementation in trapped-ion systems, and it contributes to the ongoing efforts to understand the role of quantum coherence and measurement in thermodynamics.

</details>


### [98] [Subspace Controllability in Variational Quantum Circuits: Maximising Expressivity and Increasing Search Efficiency with Dynamical Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24475)
*Andrew Rowan Barlow,Hans-Martin Rieser,Markus Lange*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文探究子空间可控性变分量子模型在经典任务（MNIST-1D多分类）中是否优于超参数搜索，发现取得最低训练/验证损失的大多数量子模型均为子空间可控模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分混合量子模型是量子硬件上实现机器学习的常见范式，但其损失景观存在固有问题导致训练困难。已有研究通过扩展子空间可控性提升了量子任务中寻找全局最小值的成功率，但这些结论是否适用于经典任务、以及相对于超参数搜索是否具有优势，尚不清楚。

Method: 在MNIST-1D多分类任务中采用可控电路，并将其与超参数搜索进行对比，考察子空间可控性对训练与验证损失的影响。

Result: 在超参数搜索的对比下，达到最低训练损失和验证损失的多数量子模型均为子空间可控模型。

Conclusion: 超参数搜索可被限制在这类子空间可控模型范围内，从而降低计算成本与时间开销。

Abstract: Variational hybrid quantum models are a common paradigm for realising machine learning on quantum hardware. Nonetheless, they are challenging to train due to several problems intrinsic to the loss landscapes formed by variational quantum circuits. To mitigate these issues, researchers have extended the idea of subspace controllability leading to an increase in the success rate of variational models in finding the global minimum for quantum-based tasks. However, it remains unknown whether these results extend to classical-based tasks, and if any advantages are realised over a hyperparameter search. We begin to address this question by employing controllable circuits in a multi- classification task using MNIST-1D. Our results show that over a hyperparameter search, the majority of quantum models that achieve the lowest training and validation losses are subspace controllable. These results indicate that the hyperparameter search can be restricted to such models, in turn reducing computational cost and time.

</details>


### [99] [Statistical mechanics of multipartite entanglement in hypergraph states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24508)
*Paolo Scarafile,Giorgia Trotta,Paolo Facchi,Giuseppe Magnifico,Giorgio Parisi,Saverio Pascazio,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过统计力学框架研究纯n量子比特超图态中的多体纠缠，将平均二分配纯度映射为2^n个经典二元自旋的有效哈密顿量，并用精确枚举与模拟退火/并行回火方法定量预测最大多体纠缠态(MMES)的数量与纠缠统计分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠在受限量子态族（如超图态）中的分布与数量难以刻画；需要一种可系统处理指数级状态空间的方法，以理解纠缠结构并获得MMES计数。

Method: 建立统计力学对应：每个超图态对应一个经典自旋构型，温度作为控制参数，在高温柔性为随机超图态均匀系综、零温为MMES；平均二分配纯度映射为2^n经典二元自旋的有效哈密顿量，零温熵的指数给出超图态集合中MMES数量。小系统(n≤5)精确枚举刻画能量景观与热力学量；大系统(n=6,7)采用模拟退火与并行回火采样。

Result: 小系统精确枚举完整刻画能量景观与热力学可观测，并验证已知MMES计数；大系统通过模拟退火与并行回火有效采样指数级状态空间，给出MMES数量的定量预测，并揭示纠缠在超图态集合中的统计分布。

Conclusion: 超图态可作为通过热力学方法研究多体纠缠的理想平台，既提供计算进展，也为受限量子态族中量子纠缠结构提供物理洞见。

Abstract: We investigate multipartite entanglement in a particular family of pure $n$-qubit hypergraph states through a statistical-mechanics framework, where the average bipartite purity maps onto an effective Hamiltonian of $2^n$ classical binary spins. In this correspondence, each hypergraph state uniquely corresponds to a classical spin configuration, while temperature serves as a control parameter that continuously interpolates between a uniform ensemble of random hypergraph states at high temperature and maximally multipartite entangled states (MMES) at zero temperature. Remarkably, the exponential of the zero-temperature entropy directly gives the number of MMES within the set of hypergraph states. For small system sizes ($n \leq 5$), we perform an exact enumeration, fully characterizing the energy landscape and associated thermodynamic observables, and validating known MMES counts. For larger systems ($n = 6$ and $7$), where exact methods become computationally infeasible, we employ simulated annealing and parallel tempering algorithms to efficiently sample the exponentially large state space. Our analysis yields quantitative predictions of the number of MMES and reveals how entanglement is statistically distributed across the sets of hypergraph states. These results establish hypergraph states as an ideal platform for investigating multipartite entanglement through thermodynamic methods, offering both computational advances and physical insights into the structure of quantum entanglement in restricted families of quantum states.

</details>


### [100] [A Reconfigurable Multilayer Quantum Key Distribution Network over Existing Metropolitan Fibre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24561)
*Mariella Minder,Andreas Siakolas,Elizabeth Pasatembou,Stylianos Mavrikos,Stephanos Yerolatsitis,Konstantinos Katzis,Kyriacos Kalli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文演示了部署于既有城域光纤上的七节点多层QKD网络，通过光谱管理、可重构光交换与网状密钥管理层，在无新增发射机条件下扩展物理连通性与业务可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络规模化受限于光纤与设备资源，尤其在"棕地"部署场景中，量子网络必须围绕既有经典电信基础设施与QKD技术的实际运行能力来设计，需解决继承性约束下的架构工程问题。

Method: 构建七节点多层QKD网络：对比多种光谱管理技术以适配各层继承约束；将可信节点环与一个由可重构光交换互连的三节点子网结合；采用网状密钥管理层，将端点请求映射到可信中继路径，为L1、L3及一次性密码本应用供钥。

Result: 73天内，8种链路配置共生成119.4 Gbit密钥材料，链路可用率99.1%；随机化端到端请求揭示密钥消耗、存储密钥状态与量子层重配置之间存在间接跨层耦合。

Conclusion: 通过可重构性、光谱复用与逻辑密钥管理抽象实现资源共�享，可在既有城域光纤上扩展量子网络的服务可达性，验证了多层QKD在此类约束环境下的可行性。

Abstract: Scaling quantum networks requires architectures extending end-to-end service reachability despite constrained fibre and equipment. Critically, in brownfield deployments, the quantum network must be engineered around classical telecommunications infrastructure and the operating capabilities of quantum key distribution (QKD) technology. To address this, we demonstrate a seven-node, multilayer QKD network deployed over existing metropolitan fibre. The implementation compares spectral management techniques to accommodate inherited constraints across its layers. It combines a trusted-node ring with a three-node subnetwork interconnected through reconfigurable optical switching, extending physical connectivity and service reachability without an additional transmitter. A meshed key management layer maps endpoint requests onto trusted-relay paths and supplies Layer 1, Layer 3, and one-time-pad applications. Over 73 days, eight link configurations generated 119.4 Gbit of secret key material with 99.1% link availability. Randomised end-to-end requests revealed indirect cross-layer coupling between key consumption, stored-key state and quantum-layer reconfiguration, demonstrating resource sharing through reconfigurability, spectral multiplexing and logical key-management abstraction.

</details>


### [101] [Explicit closed-form propagators for time-dependent gain-loss master equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24617)
*Léonce Dupays*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对含时速率主方程中的时序问题，本文给出了生成元为 $\mathcal{L}(t)=\alpha(t)A+\beta(t)B$、且 $[A,B]$ 为 $A$ 和 $B$ 伴随作用的共同本征算符情形下时序/逆时序传播子的显式闭式因子分解，并应用于玻色增益-损耗 Lindblad 方程得到任意含时增益与损耗速率的精确动力学映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时速率主方程中的时序（time ordering）是驱动开放系统动力学的核心障碍。已有的 Wei-Norman 分解等解纠缠方法需要通过求解耦合非线性微分方程组来确定分解系数，过程繁琐。本文旨在找到一种无需解非线性微分方程、可直接确定因子分解系数的构造方法，从而获得含时驱动开放系统的精确可解动力学。

Method: 当 $\mathcal{L}(t)=\alpha(t)A+\beta(t)B$ 且对易子 $[A,B]$ 同时是 $A$、$B$ 伴随作用的共同本征算符（$[A,[A,B]]=\lambda_A[A,B]$，$[B,[A,B]]=\lambda_B[A,B]$）时，直接构造时序与逆时序传播子的闭式因子分解，避免 Wei-Norman 方法中的非线性耦合微分方程组。将该结果应用于玻色增益-损耗 Lindblad 方程，求任意含时增益率和损耗率下的精确动力学映射，用于描述与热库耦合的驱动谐振子。

Result: 获得了任意含时增益与损耗速率下玻色增益-损耗主方程的精确动力学映射。通过计算初始 Fock 态 Wigner 函数的时间演化，并与独立的相空间结果对比，验证了该构造的正确性。进一步将所得动力学映射用于评估二项式玻色码在驱动增益-损耗动力学下的码空间存活概率。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭式因子分解方法为含时驱动开放系统提供了直接、无需非线性微分方程求解的精确可解途径，并在玻色增益-损耗模型中展示了从 Wigner 函数演化到玻色码存活概率等具体应用，表明该框架对量子驱动耗散系统分析具有实用价值。

Abstract: Time ordering is a central obstacle in driven open-system dynamics governed by master equations with time-dependent rates. We derive explicit closed-form factorizations of the chronological and antichronological propagators for a time-dependent generator of the form $\mathcal{L}(t)=α(t)A+β(t)B$, when the commutator $[A,B]$ is a simultaneous eigenoperator of the adjoint actions of $A$ and $B$, namely $[A,[A,B]]=λ_{A}[A,B]$ and $[B,[A,B]]=λ_{B}[A,B]$. In contrast to the disentangling procedure known as the Wei-Norman decomposition, our construction determines the factorization coefficients directly, without solving a coupled system of nonlinear differential equations. Applying this result to the bosonic gain-loss Lindblad equation, we obtain the exact dynamical map for arbitrary time-dependent gain and loss rates. The gain-loss master equation provides a canonical description of a driven harmonic oscillator coupled to a thermal bath. We benchmark the construction by deriving the time evolution of the Wigner function of an initial Fock state and comparing it with independent phase-space results. We further apply the resulting dynamical map to evaluate the code-space survival probability of a binomial bosonic code under driven gain-loss dynamics.

</details>


### [102] [Phase space anatomy of dynamical quantum phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24636)
*Zakaria Mzaouali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用离散相空间证明，动力学量子相变中"序参量转变"与"回返率非解析"是两种本质不同、所需信息尺度不同的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献将动力学量子相变（DQPT）通常以晚期序参量行为或量子态回返率的非解析性来定义，但尚未厘清这两种定义之间的关系与区别。

Method: 采用离散相空间（discrete phase space）框架，比较序参量转变与全局回返所需的信息来源；针对 stabilizer 回返，推导回返率的精确分解，分离出"支撑损失（loss of support）"与"破坏性量子干涉（destructive quantum interference）"两类代价；并以 qutrit Potts 链的动力学和精确控制作为示例。

Result: 序参量转变由局部约化态决定，不要求准概率负性（quasiprobability negativity）；而一般全局回返可携带任何真约化态都不含有的信息。qutrit Potts 链显示选择性相消可反转竞争性分块回返的排序并延迟其交换；一个精确控制还表现出在交叉点处零负性的热力学回返尖点。

Conclusion: 回返奇异性的存在性与干涉在选择其分支中的作用是彼此独立的物理问题；离散相空间从根本上将 DQPT 的两种定义按其所需信息尺度区分开来。

Abstract: Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPT) are commonly defined either by the behavior of a late-time order parameter or by nonanalyticities in a quantum state's return rate. We show that discrete phase space fundamentally separates these two notions by the scale of information they require. An order parameter transition is determined by a local reduced state, requiring no quasiprobability negativity. In contrast, general global returns can contain information absent from every proper reduced state. For stabilizer returns, we derive an exact decomposition of the rate into distinct costs from loss of support and destructive quantum interference. Dynamics of a qutrit Potts chain show that selective cancellation can reverse the ranking of competing block returns and delay their exchange. An exact control also exhibits a thermodynamic return cusp with zero negativity at the crossing. The existence of a return singularity and the role of interference in selecting its branches are therefore distinct physical questions.

</details>


### [103] [Circuit Hypernetworks for Quantum-Augmented Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24657)
*Xiaoqiang Wang,Mengyang Xiong,Jun Dai,Bang Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: HyperQ在冻结的掩码扩散语言模型中引入token条件化的量子残差分支，仅训练附加分支，使16–64量子比特电路可在11亿参数骨干内训练，下游平均分从47.65提升至54.30。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可通过改变逐token的计算方式进行适配，量子电路提供了这一途径，但在大模型内部评估较大规模量子电路的计算开销过高，因此需要一种可扩展的量子增强架构。

Method: 在每个transformer块中增加量子残差分支：读取token隐状态，输出该token电路的坐标，执行电路，并将测量值经残差连接加回。骨干保持冻结，仅训练新增分支。分支内由轻量级电路超网络在共享稀疏电路结构上生成token特定的旋转角、耦合强度与测量轴；所需期望值具有精确经典表达式，其评估成本随量子比特数线性增长。

Result: 量子比特宽度增加使下游基准平均分从47.65升至54.30；64量子比特时，HyperQ分别超过骨干模型与低秩适配（LoRA）版本4.71分与3.67分。HyperQ仅在20,000条提示-响应对上微调，而经典基线使用200,000条。

Conclusion: 结果支持“token条件化电路生成”作为一种可行的架构路径，用于实现量子增强的语言建模。

Abstract: Language models can be adapted by changing the computations applied to individual tokens. Quantum circuits offer one such approach, but evaluating wider circuits inside a large model can be computationally demanding. Here we introduce HyperQ, which adds token-conditioned quantum residual branches to a frozen masked-diffusion language model. A quantum residual branch is a module in each transformer block that reads a token's hidden state, emits the coordinates of that token's circuit, executes it, and adds the measured values back through a residual connection. The backbone remains frozen, and only the added branches are trained. Within each branch, a lightweight circuit hypernetwork emits token-specific rotation angles, coupling strengths, and measurement axes in a shared sparse circuit structure. The required expectation values have an exact classical expression whose evaluation cost grows linearly with the qubit count, enabling circuits from 16 to 64 qubits to be trained within a 1.1-billion-parameter backbone. Across downstream benchmarks, increasing circuit width raises the average score from 47.65 to 54.30. At 64 qubits, HyperQ exceeds the backbone and its low-rank-adapted counterpart by 4.71 and 3.67 points, respectively. HyperQ is fine-tuned on 20,000 prompt-response pairs, compared with 200,000 for the classical baselines. These findings support token-conditioned circuit emission as a tractable architectural approach to quantum-augmented language modelling.

</details>


### [104] [Experimental evidence of generalization in quantum machine learning in small-data regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24666)
*Leena Anthony,Artemiy Burov,Nicolas Piro,Matteo Dal Peraro,Clément Javerzac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了硬件兼容、含中段测量与前馈的QCNN，验证其在极少样本下仍具泛化能力，同时揭示量子卷积网络扩展的主要瓶颈来自数据编码与硬件执行而非优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习被视为在医学影像、临床试验、罕见病等数据受限场景中学习的有前景范式。QCNN因层次化结构、强归纳偏置及参数量随系统规模仅对数增长而受关注，其吸引力基于Caro等人的泛化界：泛化误差随可训练参数数量而非希尔伯特空间维度扩展。作者希望检验QCNN在实践中是否真能实现少样本学习，并识别其扩展瓶颈。

Method: 开发硬件兼容的QCNN，采用中段测量（mid-circuit measurement）与经典前馈；在二值手写数字任务上进行少样本测试；在匹配45参数预算下与小型经典卷积网络对比，并参照约25,000参数的无约束经典基线；对振幅编码与角度编码电路在2x2至512x512像素分辨率下进行转译，分析深度与量子比特数的扩展特性；在BreastMNIST基准上评估。

Result: 二值手写数字任务中，仅10个训练样本即可获得较强测试性能，且泛化误差随训练集增大而下降；在45参数匹配预算下QCNN能学到有效模式，而同等小型经典卷积网络停留在随机水平，但数据充足时约25,000参数的无约束经典基线最强。转译分析显示：振幅编码量子比特高效但电路深度急剧增长，角度编码深度浅但量子比特需求过高。在BreastMNIST上QCNN未超过无约束经典网络，但以少几个数量级的参数稳定高于随机水平。

Conclusion: 对QCNN而言，少样本学习在实践中可实现；而扩展到现实图像数据时，主要制约因素不是优化，而是数据编码与硬件执行。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning is a promising paradigm for learning from limited data, a central bottleneck in domains such as medical imaging, clinical trials, and rare diseases. Quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) are particularly attractive in this setting, combining a hierarchical architecture with strong inductive bias and a parameter count that grows only logarithmically with system size. Their appeal rests on the generalization bounds of Caro et al. (2022), which show that the generalization error of a quantum model scales with the number of trainable parameters rather than with the Hilbert-space dimension, placing QCNNs in a potentially sample-efficient regime. We develop a hardware-compatible QCNN with mid-circuit measurement and classical feed-forward, and show on a binary handwritten-digit task that strong test performance is achievable from as few as 10 training samples, with the generalization error decreasing as the training set grows. At a matched 45-parameter budget the QCNN learns where an equally small classical convolutional network stays at chance, although an unconstrained classical baseline with roughly 25,000 parameters remains strongest when data are plentiful. Transpiling amplitude and angle encoded circuits across image resolutions from 2x2 to 512x512 pixels then exposes the dominant scaling bottleneck: amplitude encoding stays qubit-efficient but grows extremely deep, whereas angle encoding stays shallow but becomes qubit-prohibitive. On the medically motivated BreastMNIST benchmark the QCNN does not surpass the unconstrained classical network, yet it learns consistently above chance using orders of magnitude fewer parameters. Our results indicate that for QCNNs, learning from few samples is attainable in practice, whereas scaling to realistic image data is constrained less by optimization than by data encoding and hardware execution.

</details>


### [105] [Wigner-positive quantum states can have lower entropy than the vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24670)
*Zacharie Van Herstraeten,Nicolas J. Cerf,Ulysse Chabaud*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构造了Wigner熵低于真空的解析反例，否定了Wigner熵猜想及更强的Wigner主序化猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wigner熵猜想认为纯高斯态最小化非负Wigner函数的Wigner熵（即真空熵为下界）。作者旨在检验该猜想及更强的Wigner主序化猜想是否成立。

Method: 将一个特定的纯Wigner负态与另一适当选取的量子态混合，使Wigner函数恢复非负性，同时保持Wigner熵略低于真空熵；基于极端非负Wigner函数的刻画构造解析反例，并分析其物理机制（不确定性原理与非高斯性的相互作用）。

Result: 构造出违反熵界不超过10^{-3}的简单解析反例，表明存在Wigner熵亚真空的态，可任意接近真空，并在0<α<2时呈现亚真空Wigner-Rényi α-熵。

Conclusion: Wigner熵猜想与Wigner主序化猜想均被否定；亚真空Wigner熵态源于不确定性原理与非高斯性之间的微妙相互作用。

Abstract: The Wigner entropy conjecture posits that pure Gaussian states minimize the Shannon entropy of non-negative Wigner functions, known as the Wigner entropy. We show that mixing a specific pure Wigner-negative state with another suitably chosen quantum state can restore Wigner positivity while retaining a Wigner entropy that is slightly - but strictly - below the vacuum entropy. As a consequence, we disprove the Wigner entropy conjecture, as well as the stronger Wigner majorization conjecture, by deriving simple analytical counterexamples with violations of the entropy bound no larger than $10^{-3}$. Such states with sub-vacuum Wigner entropy can be found arbitrarily close to the vacuum and can also exhibit sub-vacuum Wigner-Rényi $α$-entropy for $0<α<2$. We identify the physical mechanism behind the existence of such states, which arises from a subtle interplay between the uncertainty principle and non-Gaussianity. The key idea of this paper was developed with the help of AI tools, building on the characterization of extreme non-negative Wigner functions.

</details>


### [106] [Unified spatiotemporal quantum states and spatiotemporal entanglement from Kirkwood-Dirac phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24680)
*Zhian Jia,Mei-Hui Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间，给出时空量子态的统一刻画，并通过(准)概率混合构造统一框架，联系OTOC与KMS条件，并分析时空纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子态概念局限于空间域，而量子事件在时空中的关联需要推广为时空量子态；现有多种时空量子态表述缺乏统一框架，且其非经典性与时间性的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 采用时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间刻画时空量子态，指出其一般非厄米、非正规，而Margenau-Hill分布构造的为厄米；通过Kirkwood-Dirac时空态的(准)概率混合实现统一；推导时空态的递归表达式，并推广到多体关联函数及s参数化时空态框架，结合Petz时间反演研究两时KMS条件。

Result: 统一了几乎现有所有时空量子态表述；揭示Kirkwood-Dirac非经典性与时空态时间性的关系；建立多体关联函数与OTOC的联系；给出s参数化时空态的Petz时间反演及两时KMS条件应用；用多种互补熵度量分析时空纠缠。

Conclusion: 时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间为时空量子态提供了统一且可推广的框架，能涵盖现有表述并拓展至OTOC、KMS条件与时空纠缠等场景。

Abstract: The notion of a spatiotemporal quantum state extends the conventional concept of a spatial quantum state to the spatiotemporal domain. Such states are represented by unit-trace operators that encode correlations among quantum events distributed across space and time. In this work, we use the spatiotemporal Kirkwood-Dirac phase space to provide a unified characterization of spatiotemporal quantum states. Spatiotemporal states obtained from Kirkwood-Dirac distributions are generally non-Hermitian and nonnormal; whereas those constructed from Margenau-Hill distributions are Hermitian. We provide a unification via (quasi)probabilistic mixture of Kirkwwod-Dirac spatiotemporal states which encompasses almost all existing formulations of spatiotemporal quantum states. We derive recursive expressions for spatiotemporal states and elucidate the relation between Kirkwood-Dirac nonclassicality and the temporality of spatiotemporal states. We further extend the construction to many-fold correlation functions and establish its connection with out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs). We also develop a more general unifying framework based on (quasi)probabilistic mixture of $s$-parametrized spatiotemporal states and establish their Petz time reversal and application in studying Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) condition in two-time setting. Finally, we apply this framework to characterize quantum entanglement in spacetime and analyze spatiotemporal entanglement using several complementary entropy measures.

</details>


### [107] [Superconducting qubit based on altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24759)
*Xue-Feng Pan,Xin-Lei Hei,Franco Nori,Peng-Bo Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用超导体-交错磁体-超导体约瑟夫森结设计可编程电流-相位关系，提出一类结合大非谐性与抗退相干能力的 transmon 型超导量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体具有零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂，为下一代约瑟夫森器件提供新平台；现有超导量子比特在保持高相干性的同时难以兼顾大非谐性与可调性，亟需新的约瑟夫森势工程手段。

Method: 在超导体-交错磁体-超导体（S-A-S）结中研究约瑟夫森效应，通过器件设计实现可编程的电流-相位关系，并基于此构造利用交错磁性的可编程约瑟夫森势，进而提出一类 transmon 型超导量子比特；在 2φ 结区分析其隧穿机制（相干双库珀对隧穿）以及磁通调控与噪声抑制特性。

Result: 该 transmon 型量子比特同时具备大非谐性和由相干双库珀对隧穿带来的抗退相干增强；在 2φ 结区通过宇称保护获得对电荷噪声的固有防护；磁场可精确调控量子比特，并在合适偏置下进一步抑制电荷噪声与磁通噪声。

Conclusion: 交错磁体可作为约瑟夫森势工程的多功能平台，为兼具高相干性、大非谐性和宽可调性的高性能超导量子比特开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Altermagnets, characterized by vanishing net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provide a promising platform for next-generation Josephson devices. Here, we exploit the Josephson effect in superconductor-altermagnet-superconductor junctions and show how to engineer prescribed current-phase relations by device design. Based on these programmable Josephson potentials utilizing altermagnetism, we propose a new class of transmon-like superconducting qubits that combine large anharmonicity with enhanced robustness against decoherence via coherent two-Cooper-pair tunneling. We show that in the $2φ$-junction regime, this kind of qubit provides intrinsic protection against charge noise due to parity protection. Magnetic flux can be used to precisely control the qubit and, under appropriate bias, this architecture further suppresses charge and flux noise. Our results establish altermagnets as a versatile platform for Josephson-potential engineering and open a new route toward high-performance superconducting qubits combining high coherence, large anharmonicity, and broad tunability.

</details>


### [108] [Observable-targeted variational quantum simulation of Hamiltonian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24794)
*Leonardo Zambrano,Luciano Pereira,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 标准变分量子模拟以复现完整量子态为目标，但许多应用只需少量可观测量。本文提出面向纯态哈密顿动力学的变分方法，通过更新电路参数直接复现所选可观测量的演化，而非全态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整量子态的变分模拟测量与优化成本高，而实际任务往往只需若干可观测量的期望值。将资源聚焦于目标可观测量的演化，有望在同等测量预算下延长精确模拟时间。

Method: 基于精确误差恒等式指导可观测量的选择，构造由目标可观测量与哈密顿量的重复对易子组成的算符集合。针对泡利可观测量与泡利旋转电路，推导出无需辅助量子比特或受控操作即可估计的更新规则，从而避免重叠度估计的额外开销。

Result: 在六比特自旋、费米子与分子基准上，相同测量次数预算下，目标化更新将中位误差容限内时间的延长量最大提升至标准变分量子模拟的4.2倍。

Conclusion: 将变分更新导向目标可观测量的策略，可在不增加每时间步测量成本的前提下显著延长精确模拟的有效时长，为资源受限的量子动力学模拟提供高效替代方案。

Abstract: Standard variational quantum simulation seeks to reproduce the evolution of the full quantum state, although many applications require only the expectation values of a few observables. We study a variational method for pure-state Hamiltonian dynamics that updates circuit parameters to reproduce the evolution of selected expectation values. An exact error identity guides the choice of observables, motivating a construction based on repeated commutators of the target with the Hamiltonian. For Pauli observables and Pauli-rotation circuits, the update can be estimated without ancillary qubits or controlled operations for overlap estimation. Across six-qubit spin, fermionic, and molecular benchmarks, the targeted update extends the median time within the target-error tolerance by up to a factor of $4.2$ relative to standard variational quantum simulation at equal shot budgets. These results show that directing the variational update toward the target observable can extend accurate simulation without increasing the measurement cost per time step.

</details>


### [109] [Stabilizing temporal quantum-enhanced sensitivity via sub-optimal measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24805)
*Wangsheng Zheng,Yaoling Yang,Abolfazl Bayat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了多参数非平衡量子传感中的普适时间标度律，揭示次优测量下精度呈二次标度并受有界振荡函数调制，并提出将测量分组在不同时刻执行的分区测量协议，从而稳定获得鲁棒的二次标度，推进量子增强灵敏度的实际实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子增强灵敏度通常以精度随时间超线性标度来衡量，但这依赖复杂且含时的最优测量，实际难以实现；次优测量往往无法保持鲁棒的超线性标度并出现振荡。因此需要研究次优测量下的普适标度行为，并寻找可实际操作的方案来稳定超线性精度。

Method: 首先在一般多参数非平衡量子传感框架下建立普适的时间标度律；在此基础上刻画次优测量时传感精度的普适行为，即由有界振荡函数调制的二次标度；进而提出分区测量协议，将测量划分为不同组并分别在不同时刻进行，以消除振荡部分，获得集体精度。

Result: 次优测量下的精度表现为二次标度并叠加有界振荡；所提出的分区测量协议即使在多参数情形下、对次优测量也能稳定出鲁棒的二次标度。该协议通过三个不同实例及贝叶斯估计得到验证。

Conclusion: 该协议适用于任何信息性测量，能够消除次优测量带来的振荡，稳定实现超线性（二次）标度，是迈向量子增强灵敏度实际应用的关键一步。

Abstract: In non-equilibrium probes, quantum-enhanced sensitivity is quantified through super-linear scaling of precision with respect to time, as a central metrology resource. However, this requires optimal measurements, which are often complex and time-dependent, making it challenging in practice. Sub-optimal measurements typically fail to retain robust super-linear scaling and show oscillations. Here, we begin by establishing a universal temporal scaling law in a general multi-parameter non-equilibrium quantum sensing framework. Within this setting, we identify the universal behavior of the sensing precision with sub-optimal measurements which shows quadratic scaling modulated by an additional bounded oscillatory function. To remove the oscillatory part, we propose a protocol in which measurements are partitioned into different groups, each performed at different times. The collective precision obtained from this protocol stabilizes a robust quadratic scaling even for sub-optimal measurements in the multi-parameter regime. We validate our protocol through three distinct examples as well as Bayesian estimation. Our protocol is applicable to every informative measurement and takes a key step towards practical realization of quantum-enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [110] [Trade-offs and experimental feasibility of nonlocal polygamy with two-outcome Bell inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24835)
*Josep Batle,Tomasz Rybotycki,Tomasz Białecki,Piotr Gawron,Adam Bednorz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多体量子系统中贝尔不等式的同时违反问题，寻找可在IBM量子硬件上演示的构型，并为多种“多夫性”（polygamous）构型推导界限与权衡关系，将量子计算机的实验能力与量子关联极限的数学方法相连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与贝尔非局域性在多体系统各子系统间的共享约束不同，而这类共享限制（尤其是不等式同时违反的可能性）缺乏系统刻画；同时需要与真实量子硬件可实现性相结合的研究。

Method: 针对每方采用二值可观测量、每方两个或三个测量设置的贝尔不等式，分析其同时违反情况，识别可在IBM Quantum硬件上实现同时违反的构型，并对若干polygamous构型推导界限与权衡关系。

Result: 识别出若干可在IBM Quantum硬件上演示同时违反的构型，并为多种polygamous构型给出了相应的界限与权衡关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果在量子计算机的实验能力与刻画量子关联极限的数学方法之间建立了联系，为多体量子关联的共享约束研究提供了理论与实验结合的路径。

Abstract: Entanglement and Bell nonlocality have different sharing constraints across subsystems of a multipartite quantum system. We examine simultaneous violations of Bell inequalities with two-outcome observables and two or three measurement settings per party. We identify configurations whose simultaneous violations can be demonstrated on IBM Quantum hardware, and derive bounds and trade-off relations for several polygamous configurations. The results connect experimental capabilities of quantum computers with mathematical methods for studying the limits of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [111] [Explanation of the Observed Energy Exchange through the vacuum in Optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24900)
*Vincenzo Macrì,Franco Nori,Alessandro Ferreri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出标准大失谐腔光力学的辐射压近似忽略了高阶镜-场相互作用，而后者可自然产生声子-声子耦合，微观解释了Fong等人（Nature 576, 243 (2019)）实验中的真空介导热传递，并据此提出可提取净功的循环协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准大失谐腔光力学仅保留辐射压相互作用、忽略高阶镜-场项，这一近似无法微观解释实验中两机械薄膜间的真空介导热传递，其物理起源长期存在争议。因此需要超越线性近似，揭示高阶相互作用的基本作用。

Method: 采用全量子模型，将此前被忽略的高阶光力学相互作用纳入，推导其导致的声子-声子耦合；并基于该微观描述设计一个由声子-声子相互作用驱动的循环过程以实现净功提取。

Result: 高阶光力学相互作用自然生成声子-声子耦合，在标准光力学框架内定量复现了实验观测到的能量交换；进一步给出了可提取净功的循环协议方案。

Conclusion: 确立了高阶光力学相互作用的基础性地位，为超越线性近似的下一代光力学实验提供了微观描述框架。

Abstract: In cavity optomechanics, the standard large-detuning regime is commonly described by retaining only the radiation-pressure interaction, while higher-order mirror--field interactions are assumed to be negligible. This approximation, however, fails to provide a microscopic explanation for the vacuum-mediated heat transfer observed between the mechanical membranes in the experiment of Fong, et.al.,[Nature \textbf{576}, 243 (2019)], whose physical origin has remained under active debate. Here, using a fully quantum model, we show that the neglected higher-order optomechanical interactions naturally generate phonon-phonon coupling and quantitatively account for the observed energy exchange within the standard optomechanical framework. Building on this microscopic description, we further propose a protocol in which phonon-phonon interaction drives a cyclic process enabling net work extraction. Our results establish the fundamental role of higher-order optomechanical interactions and provide a microscopic framework for describing next-generation optomechanical experiments beyond the linear approximation.

</details>


### [112] [A thermal microwave bus for neutral atom quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24933)
*Matthew J. H. Kendall,Christopher J. Watson,Michael Ben Shem,Jonathan D. Breeze*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出四能级架构，利用微波腔耦合里德伯态实现长程腔介导门，并证明其可加速量子纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列中高保真两比特门依赖里德伯阻塞，但阻塞本质上是短程的，需通过原子穿梭实现长程连接，代价高且限制扩展性。作者希望借助微波腔提供长程相互作用，同时保留基态比特的长寿命优势。

Method: 设计四能级架构：基态比特用于长寿命存储，里德伯态与微波腔耦合以实现长程门。先分析两种既有协议（Tavis-Cummings模型的色散iSWAP、驱动腔产生的受控相位门）的热依赖性，并模拟里德伯衰减、热光子、有限腔线宽，给出闭合形式的保真度上界。随后提出双色拉曼门，仅虚占据里德伯态并抵消色散位移，以抑制原子和腔衰减。最后将腔介导门用于光学镊阵列，闭合环面码及双变量自行车码的周期边界。

Result: 双色拉曼门达到保真度F=0.997。在真实阵列尺寸下，用腔介导门闭合环面码周期边界，使纠错轮次缩短2.3倍；推广到双变量自行车码族，使gross code的一轮纠错缩短4.8倍。

Conclusion: 腔介导的长程门可在中性原子阵列中同时兼顾基态比特长寿命与里德伯耦合，双色拉曼方案显著提升保真度，并在多种量子纠错码上大幅缩短纠错轮次，展示其在可扩展量子计算中的潜力。

Abstract: High-fidelity two-qubit gates in neutral-atom arrays rely on the Rydberg blockade, which is intrinsically short ranged and requires long range connectivity to be achieved through atom shuttling. We propose a four level architecture, where the typical ground state qubit can be leveraged for its long lifetime and the Rydberg states couple to a microwave cavity, allowing for long range cavity mediated gates. We first find the thermal dependence of two established protocols, the dispersive iSWAP native to the Tavis-Cummings model and the Controlled phase gate generated from driving the cavity. We simulate them under the presence of Rydberg decay, thermal photons, finite cavity linewidth and find fidelities which accompany closed form bounds. We then introduce the bichromatic Raman gate, which only virtually populates the Rydberg states and cancels dispersive shifts to mitigate both atomic and cavity decay, achieving a fidelity of $F = 0.997$. Finally, we consider a full optical tweezer array in a microwave cavity, and show that using a cavity mediated gate to close the periodic boundaries of the toric code shortens an error correction round by a factor of 2.3 for realistic array sizes. This was then applied to the wider family of Bivariate bicycle codes, and we find that it shortens a round of the gross code by a factor of 4.8.

</details>


### [113] [Efficient Heralding of Loss-Tolerant Photonic GHZ States for Device-Independent Conference Key Agreement over Long Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24941)
*Yazeed K. Alwehaibi,Makoto Ishihara,Shakib Daryanoosh,Ewan Mer,Shang Yu,Wojciech Roga,Ian A. Walmsley,Masahiro Takeoka,Raj B. Patel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究损失量子网络中设备无关会议密钥协商（DI-CKA）所需的预示多体纠缠分发，提出计算基真空-n光子GHZ态比固定光子数GHZ态具有更低临界探测效率，并给出星型网络异构源协议实现该态的直接预示方案，兼顾长距离扩展与当前探测器可实现的DI-CKA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预示多体纠缠是损耗量子网络中DI-CKA的关键需求。虽然无损耗时不同单轨光子数编码的GHZ态局域等价，但在有损条件下其损耗容忍度差异显著，因此需要从编码、源架构与测量角色分配等设计参数入手，寻找更利于实际探测效率与网络扩展的方案。

Method: 1）比较计算基GHZ态（真空与n光子分量相干叠加）与固定光子数GHZ态，推导两类态的临界探测效率解析条件；2）提出使用异构源的星型网络协议，直接预示真空-n光子GHZ态，给出长距离扩展标度O(η_c^{n/2})；3）针对四用户刻画损耗下预示态，证明可调源参数在任意有限距离保持真正多体纠缠；4）分析理想Pauli测量与位移基测量的DI-CKA表现，并评估基于SPDC的实现及源致不对称对parity-CHSH测量角色分配的影响。

Result: 计算基真空-n光子GHZ态可在显著更低探测效率下实现无探测漏洞的parity-CHSH违背，临界探测效率优于固定光子数GHZ态；四用户协议在任意有限信道距离保持真正多体纠缠；在理想Pauli测量与实验可及的位移基测量下均可在当前光电探测器可达效率实现DI-CKA，且密钥率与通信距离与先前预示方案相当；SPDC实现中源致不对称使parity-CHSH测试的测量角色分配至关重要。

Conclusion: 光子数编码、源架构和测量角色分配是损耗容忍多体量子网络与增强DI-CKA性能的关键设计参数；真空-n光子编码结合异构源星型网络可在实际探测条件下实现更优的DI-CKA。

Abstract: Heralded multipartite entanglement distribution is a key requirement for device-independent conference key agreement (DI-CKA) over lossy quantum networks. Although locally equivalent in the absence of loss, different single-rail photon-number encodings of Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states can exhibit substantially different loss tolerance. We show that computational-basis GHZ states, comprising a coherent superposition of vacuum and an $n$-photon component, enable detection-loophole-free parity-CHSH violations at markedly lower detection efficiencies than previously considered fixed-photon-number GHZ states, and derive exact analytical conditions for the critical detection efficiencies of both state classes. Motivated by this advantage, we introduce a star-network protocol using heterogeneous sources to directly herald vacuum-$n$-photon GHZ states with long-distance scaling $O(η_{\mathrm{c}}^{n/2})$, where $η_{\mathrm{c}}$ is the channel transmittance. For four users, we characterize the heralded state under photon loss and show that tunable source parameters preserve genuine multipartite entanglement at any finite channel distance. For both ideal Pauli measurements and experimentally accessible displacement-based measurements, our protocol enables DI-CKA at detection efficiencies achievable with current photodetectors, while retaining key rates and communication distances comparable to those of previous heralded schemes. We discuss physical implementations and analyze an SPDC-based realization, showing that source-induced asymmetry can make measurement-role assignment in the parity-CHSH test crucial. These results identify photon-number encoding, source architecture, and measurement-role assignment as design parameters for loss-tolerant multipartite quantum networks and enhanced DI-CKA performance.

</details>


### [114] [Tight entropy contraction beyond detailed balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24953)
*Li Gao,Jingyu Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为非细致平衡的有限维量子信道建立了定量熵收缩界：对与忠实条件期望相容的信道，证明GNS范数收缩可导出相对熵收缩，仅损失一个关于维数常数的对数因子；对具有忠实渐近条件期望的量子Markov半群，由此给出完全修正对数Sobolev常数下界与GNS谱隙的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子熵收缩理论多依赖细致平衡（可逆性）假设，适用范围受限。作者希望突破这一限制，研究更一般的有限维量子信道与量子Markov半群的熵收缩行为，并刻画熵收缩与谱隙之间的定量关系。

Method: 以GNS（Gelfand–Naimark–Segal）范数收缩为出发点，针对与忠实条件期望相容的信道，推导相对熵收缩界，并分析其中维数常数的对数损失；将该结果应用于具有忠实渐近条件期望的量子Markov半群，将完全修正对数Sobolev常数与GNS谱隙联系起来。

Result: 证明GNS范数收缩可推出相对熵收缩，且仅有关于维数常数的对数损失；对量子Markov半群给出完全修正对数Sobolev常数由GNS谱隙控制的下界；所得界允许初始态与外部环境纠缠，且不依赖于环境系统尺寸；在交替储备池脉冲与连续耦合能量阶梯等模型上给出具有最优尺寸依赖性的界。

Conclusion: 该工作将熵收缩理论推广至非细致平衡情形，建立了GNS范数收缩与相对熵收缩的定量联系，并给出量子Markov半群中完全修正对数Sobolev常数的谱隙下界；结果对环境纠缠与系统尺寸具有鲁棒性，并在具体物理模型中达到最优尺寸依赖性。

Abstract: We establish quantitative entropy contraction bounds for finite-dimensional quantum channels beyond detailed balance. For channels compatible with a faithful conditional expectation, we show that GNS (Gelfand--Naimark--Segal) norm contraction yields relative entropy contraction with only a logarithmic loss in the dimensional constant. For quantum Markov semigroups with a faithful asymptotic conditional expectation, this gives a lower bound on the complete modified logarithmic Sobolev constant in terms of the GNS spectral gap. The bounds allow initial entanglement with an external environment and are independent of its system size. Applications to alternating reservoir pulses and continuously coupled energy ladders give bounds with optimal size dependence.

</details>

Zhang,J. Zhao,M. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb实验利用13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中W→μν_μ衰变数据，测量了W玻色子横向螺旋度分数随其快度的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前W玻色子横向螺旋度分数在forward区域的测量缺失，需要实验数据检验标准模型在forward区的预测。

Method: 使用LHCb实验在√s=13 TeV下采集的5.1 fb⁻¹积分亮度数据，通过W→μν_μ衰变道，对μ子横动量和赝快度进行模板拟合，提取横向螺旋度分数。

Result: 测量了0≤y_W≤5范围内横向螺旋度分数随W玻色子快度的变化，结果显示强烈的快度依赖性，并与次领头阶标准模型预

Conclusion: 该测量首次给出了forward区域W玻色子横向螺旋度分数的测定结果，结果与NLO标准模型预

Abstract: The transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons are measured as a function of the $W$ boson rapidity, $y_{W}$, in the range $0 \leq y_{W} \leq 5$ using $W\toμν_μ$ decays in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded by the LHCb experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.1$ fb$^{-1}$. The fractions are extracted from a template fit to the muon transverse momentum and pseudorapidity. The results show a strong rapidity dependence and agree with next-to-leading-order Standard Model predictions, providing the first determination of the transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons in the forward region.

</details>


### [116] [Benchmarking Nucleon Production in Hadron Interactions Relevant for GeV-scale Neutrino Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22662)
*Richard Diurba,Steve Dytman,Matthew King,Yinrui Liu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用GENIE中的Geant4 Bertini Cascade与INCL++模型，研究中微子实验中常见强子（核子与π介子）在核内传播产生的核子产额，发现无介子强子相互作用的发射核子数可用简单高斯与指数衰减函数参数化，并提供针对可见能量与末态核子多重数模型差异的重加权工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子散射实验中，末态粒子数与原初中微子能量重建依赖核内强子传播的准确模拟；但不同强子-核散射模型对末态粒子数与可探测能量沉积的预测存在差异（模型spread），需要量化并可控地处理这种不确定性。

Method: 使用GENIE中微子事件发生器，对其中实现的Geant4 Bertini Cascade与INCL++两种强子散射模型进行模拟比较；针对常见靶核，考察核子与π介子诱导的强子-核相互作用，并用高斯与指数衰减函数对无介子强子相互作用发射的核子数进行参数化拟合，同时构建重加权工具。

Result: 对于常用靶核，无介子强子相互作用的发射核子数可由简单高斯和指数衰减函数很好地参数化；给出了相应关系式，并实现了可用于调整可见能量与末态核子多重数模型差异的重加权工具。

Conclusion: 强子-核散射模型对末态核子产额的影响可用简洁解析形式描述，所提出的参数化与重加权方法为中微子实验中评估和控制系统性模型不确定性提供了实用手段。

Abstract: In neutrino scattering experiments, the emitted final state particles are essential for identifying interaction channels and reconstructing neutrino energies. During intranuclear propagation, neutrino-induced hadrons can interact with surrounding nucleons, altering both the number of final state particles and detectable energy depositions. As a result, neutrino experiments rely on hadron-nucleus scattering models to simulate these nuclear effects. This paper explores nucleon production predicted by hadron scattering models for common hadrons in neutrino experiments, specifically nucleons and pions. We investigate these models with simulations from the \textsc{GENIE} neutrino event generator, which includes implementations of \textsc{Geant4} Bertini Cascade and \textsc{INCL++}. For popular target nuclei, we find that the number of emitted nucleons for mesonless hadron interactions can be parameterized by simple Gaussian and exponential decay functions. We present these relationships and provide reweighting tools for addressing the hadron scattering model spread in both visible energy and final state nucleon multiplicities.

</details>


### [117] [Mixture Density Networks for Neutrino Reconstruction at Hadron Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22979)
*Seungjin Yang,Jason S. H. Lee,Junghwan Goh*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出 MONSTER，一种基于 Transformer 事件编码器的混合密度网络，用于半轻子 t t̄ 事件中中微子动量的重建，在公开基准上优于 νflows 基线且推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子对撞机中中微子纵向动量无法直接观测，导致其动量重建存在本质歧义；现有基于归一化流的方法（νflows）虽有成效，但推理速度较慢，需要探索更具竞争力的替代方案。

Method: MONSTER（Mixture of Neutrino Solutions with Transformer Event Representation）采用基于 Transformer 的事件编码器，构建混合密度网络，对中微子动量的条件分布预测多元正态混合模型，并利用闭式密度给出无需采样的点估计。

Result: 在 νflows 公开基准上，相比经验众数 νflows 基线，MONSTER 将三动量残差的第 68 百分位降低 5%、第 95 百分位降低 6%；在偏置相当的情况下，各变量均方根误差降低 2% 至 9%；推理速度提升 2 至 8.5 倍（取决于设备与批大小）。

Conclusion: 混合密度网络是归一化流在中微子动量重建任务上的一个有竞争力的替代方案。

Abstract: Neutrino momentum reconstruction at hadron colliders is intrinsically ambiguous because the longitudinal momentum is not directly observed. We study this problem in semileptonic $t\bar{t}$ events using \monster{} (Mixture of Neutrino Solutions with Transformer Event Representation), a mixture density network that predicts a multivariate normal mixture for the conditional distribution of neutrino momentum from reconstructed event objects. The model uses a Transformer-based event encoder and yields a sampling-free point estimate of the neutrino momentum from the closed-form density. On the public benchmark introduced with \nuflows, \monster{} reduces the 68th percentile of the three-momentum residual by 5\% and the 95th percentile by 6\% relative to the empirical-mode \nuflows{} baseline, with per-variable root-mean-square errors smaller by 2 to 9\% at comparable bias, while running 2 to 8.5 times faster at inference, depending on the device and batch size. These results indicate that mixture density networks are a competitive alternative to normalizing flows for neutrino reconstruction.

</details>


### [118] [Detailed investigation on the Geant4 simulation of alpha particles in liquid scintillator detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23793)
*Shiwen Xu,Shubing Liu,Zeyuan Yu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文使用 Geant4 11.4.2 对 LAB 基液闪中 $^{214}$Po、$^{212}$Po、$^{210}$Po α 粒子能量沉积进行模拟与验证，发现 δ 电子涨落主导本征分辨率，且产生阈值的影响大于电磁模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α 粒子（来自 $^{214}$Po、$^{212}$Po、$^{210}$Po）日益用于大型液闪探测器刻度，但其能量沉积的 Geant4 模拟缺乏系统验证，需评估模拟参数对淬灭响应的影响。

Method: 在 4 m 立方 LAB 基液闪中模拟 α 粒子，采用 Geant4 11.4.2 并逐步施加 Birks 淬灭；研究步长、产生阈值、Birks 常数和电磁模型依赖；并用 Geant4-DNA 考察 keV 尺度电子径迹结构。

Result: δ 电子相关涨落主导模拟响应；在测试的显式 δ 设置下，次级径迹淬灭响应贡献的方差为总方差的 1.01–1.12 倍，负的原级—次级协方差降低总方差。淬灭曲线形状与 SNO+ 数据在当前不确定度内相符，$k_B=0.007$–$0.010$ g cm$^{-2}$ MeV$^{-1}$ 时 $χ^2/\mathrm{ndf}\simeq0.14$–$0.17$。Geant4-DNA 显示强电子径迹弯曲，迂曲度从 200 eV 约 6 降至 10 keV 的 1.6。

Conclusion: δ 电子涨落是所测配置下本征分辨率的主导贡献；产生阈值的影响大于电磁模型，Penelope 与 Livermore 在 L/E 上约 3% 内一致，而 Standard 给出更高 L/E，尤其在低阈值时。

Abstract: Purpose: Alpha particles from $^{214}$Po, $^{212}$Po, and $^{210}$Po are increasingly used to calibrate large liquid scintillator detectors, but Geant4 simulations of their energy deposition require detailed validation.
  Methods: We simulate alpha particles in a 4 m cube of LAB-based liquid scintillator with Geant4 11.4.2 and apply Birks quenching per step. We study step-size, production-threshold, Birks-constant, and electromagnetic-model dependence, and use Geant4-DNA to examine keV-scale electron track structure.
  Results: Delta-electron-associated fluctuations dominate the simulated response. Across the tested explicit-delta settings, the variance of the quenched-response contribution from secondary tracks is 1.01--1.12 times the total variance; a negative primary--secondary covariance reduces the total. The simulated quenching-curve shape is compatible with SNO+ data within current uncertainties, with $χ^2/\mathrm{ndf}\simeq0.14$--$0.17$ for $k_B=0.007$--$0.010$ g cm$^{-2}$ MeV$^{-1}$. Geant4-DNA gives strong electron-track curvature, with tortuosity decreasing from about 6 at 200 eV to 1.6 at 10 keV.
  Conclusion: Delta-electron fluctuations are the dominant contribution to the intrinsic resolution in the tested configurations. The production threshold has a larger effect than the electromagnetic model: Penelope and Livermore agree within about 3% in L/E, whereas Standard gives higher L/E, especially at low thresholds.

</details>


### [119] [Jevons' Paradox and Fast Generative Simulation for HEP: Why Realistic Benchmarking is Essential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24306)
*Thorsten Buss,Henry Day-Hall,Frank Gaede,Gregor Kasieczka,Katja Krüger,Anatolii Korol,Thomas Madlener,Peter McKeown,Martina Mozzanica,Lorenzo Valente*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 高能物理中量能器模拟是主要计算开销，快速生成模型可实现约100倍加速，但需警惕杰文斯悖论并采用真实指标衡量"真正"效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中的模拟是主要计算开销，量能器模拟尤其主导了物理分析的整体能耗。未来探测器量能器粒度更细、所需模拟统计量空前庞大，传统模拟难以支撑，因此亟需更高效的方法。

Method: 采用快速生成式模型（fast generative models）替代传统模拟，重构量能器模拟流程以提升效率。

Result: 快速生成模型能够生成比传统方法效率高约100倍的模拟数据。

Conclusion: 论文探讨该领域的杰文斯悖论（Jevons' paradox），并强调采用贴近实际的评价指标，才能真正实现效率提升，而非被需求增长所抵消。

Abstract: Simulation is a major computational expense in HEP, and calorimeter simulation in particular drives the overall energy cost of our physics analyses. Future detectors will contain more finely grained calorimeters than ever, and their data analyses will demand unprecedented simulated statistics. Fast generative models redefine what is possible, producing simulations 100 times more efficiently. This article addresses Jevons' paradox in our field and considers the importance of realistic metrics in achieving "true" efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [The detector system of the SHiP/NA67 experiment at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24378)
*Matei Climescu,on behalf of the SHiP Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SHiP/NA67实验将在CERN SPS的HI-ECN3束流设施上搜寻GeV量级的微弱相互作用新粒子，并开展全味中微子物理测量。目前合作组正优化实验的初始配置，以服务于2032-2033年的调试与首批物理运行。探测器系统由若干大面积仪器构成：用于新粒子搜寻的复杂否决探测器、高时间分辨探测器、轻量秸秆径迹系统和高校准精度量能器；用于中微子重建的小横向尺寸、高粒度量能器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GeV能标下搜寻微弱相互作用的新粒子，并利用束流设施开展全味中微子物理测量，探索超越标准模型的新物理及中微子振荡等前沿问题。

Method: 依托CERN SPS的HI-ECN3束流设施，构建SHiP/NA67探测器系统：采用大面积否决探测器、高时间分辨探测器、轻量秸秆径迹系统及高校准精度量能器进行新粒子搜寻；并采用小横向尺寸、高粒度量能器进行中微子重建。当前重点为优化2032-2033年调试与首批物理运行的初始配置。

Result: 该摘要为实验方案与现状描述，尚未给出具体物理结果；重点是探测器子系统设计及初始配置的优化进展。

Conclusion: SHiP/NA67实验通过多类大面积、高精度探测器组合，将同时支持新粒子搜寻与全味中微子物理测量，其初始配置优化为2032-2033年的调试和首批物理运行奠定基础。

Abstract: The SHiP/NA67 experiment aims to search for feebly interacting GeV-scale new particles and to perform all-flavour neutrino-physics measurements at the HI-ECN3 beam facility at the CERN SPS. The collaboration is currently optimising the experiment's initial configuration for the commissioning and first physics runs of 2032-2033. The detector subsystems comprise a few large-area instruments: for new-particle searches, highly sophisticated veto detectors, high timing-resolution detectors, a lightweight straw tracker system, and high-spatial-precision calorimeters; and, for neutrino reconstruction, small transverse-size, high-granularity calorimeters.

</details>


### [121] [mkFit for track fitting with the CMS Phase-2 detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24659)
*Leonardo Giannini,Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: mkFit算法已被CMS采纳为Run 3主径迹构建算法，实现约3.5倍加速。由于径迹拟合随之为显著耗时环节，作者将mkFit扩展至径迹拟合任务，并基于Phase-2模拟给出HLT中径迹拟合的初步物理与计算性能结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mkFit在径迹构建上带来约3.5倍加速后，径迹拟合成为整体径迹重建中占比相当的环节；同时HL-LHC对计算资源的需求进一步提升，因此有必要将并行化的mkFit扩展到径迹拟合任务以获得额外加速。

Method: 将mkFit从原有基于Kalman滤波的径迹构建，扩展至径迹拟合任务，并利用线程级与数据级并行性；在CMS高能级触发器（HLT）场景下，基于现实的Phase-2模拟进行测试。

Result: 给出了在CMS高能级触发器中使用mkFit进行径迹拟合的初步结果，涵盖物理性能与计算性能两方面，基于现实的Phase-2模拟数据。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明将mkFit扩展到径迹拟合具有可行性，是应对HL-LHC计算需求、在构建加速基础上进一步提升整体径迹重建效率的潜在途径。

Abstract: The mkFit algorithm provides an implementation of the Kalman filter-based track reconstruction algorithm that exploits both thread- and data-level parallelism. It has been adopted by the CMS Collaboration as the primary track building algorithm during the Run 3 of the LHC for both the online and offline track reconstruction sequences. Thanks to mkFit, an average speedup of a factor 3.5 is achieved in track building while retaining or improving physics performance. As a consequence of the speedup provided by mkFit in track building, track fitting has become a comparably significant component of the overall tracking time. Given the increased demands of the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, further speedup can be achieved by extending mkFit to the track fitting task. We present preliminary results for track fitting in the CMS High-Level Trigger using mkFit, covering both the physics and the computational performance, based on realistic Phase-2 simulations.

</details>


### [122] [Goodness-of-fit for multi-distribution neutrino cross-section measurements with shared events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24796)
*Francisco Martinez Lopez,London Cooper-Troendle,Steven Gardiner,Patrick Green,Tanaz Mohayai*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 从同一样本提取多个微分截面时，事件共享导致分箱计数存在精确线性约束，使协方差矩阵秩亏；作者提出"range-projected χ²"统计量，将拟合限制在独立统计信息子空间，恢复正确的自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个微分截面分布共享同一事件样本，同一事件同时贡献于多个分布，形成事件共享结构，带来分箱计数间的精确线性约束。该约束使协方差矩阵秩亏。数值精度误差可能意外恢复可逆性，但沿受约束维度的χ²贡献变成任意值，在全局拟合优度检验中产生非物理的χ²膨胀，因此需要一种正确处理该秩亏的检验统计量。

Method: 提出range-projected χ²统计量，将拟合优度检验限制在承载独立统计信息的子空间，得到自由度为N_bins − N_null的χ²，其中N_null为独立约束数。论证该秩亏是事件共享结构的可预测结果，并将N_null分解为两部分：由分箱几何先验确定的结构贡献，以及依赖相空间占据情况的运动学贡献。

Result: 验证包含两个层次：一是解析玩具模型，二是模拟的中微子–氩微分截面测量，后者涵盖解卷（unfolding）与多来源系统不确定度。结果表明range-projected χ²能正确处理秩亏，避免χ²的非物理膨胀。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵的秩亏源于事件共享结构，是可预测的；range-projected χ²通过限制到独立统计信息子空间给出正确自由度，适用于含解卷与多源系统误差的真实测量分析。

Abstract: When multiple differential cross-section measurements are extracted from a common event sample, the same events contribute simultaneously to multiple distributions. This event-sharing structure imposes exact linear constraints among the bin counts, reducing the effective dimensionality of the measurement below the total number of bins. Since the covariance matrix obeys these inter-distribution constraints, it contains a rank deficiency. While limited numerical precision may inadvertently restore invertibility, the $χ^{2}$ contributions along constrained dimensions will be arbitrary, yielding unphysically inflated $χ^{2}$ values in global goodness-of-fit tests. We present the range-projected $χ^{2}$, a test statistic that restricts the goodness-of-fit test to the subspace carrying independent statistical information, yielding a $χ^{2}$ with $N_\text{bins} - N_\text{null}$ degrees of freedom, where $N_\text{null}$ is the number of independent constraints. We show that this rank deficiency is a predictable consequence of the event-sharing structure, and that $N_\text{null}$ decomposes into a structural contribution determined a priori from the binning geometry and a kinematic contribution that depends on the phase-space occupancy. The method is validated with an analytical toy model and a simulated neutrino--argon cross-section measurement including unfolding and multi-source systematic uncertainties.

</details>


### [123] [Search for a top-philic heavy resonance in association with top quarks in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = $ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24950)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS在13/13.6 TeV pp数据中搜索与顶夸克伴随产生并衰变为ttbar的top-philic Z'玻色子，未发现显著超出，质量低于1150 GeV被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验top-philic Z'玻色子这一新物理模型，寻找与顶夸克伴随产生并衰变为顶夸克对的共振信号。

Method: 利用ATLAS在√s=13 TeV（140 fb⁻¹）和13.6 TeV（56 fb⁻¹）的pp碰撞数据，选取同号双轻子或至少三轻子事件，采用以Z'质量为参数的transformer分类器优化信号与本底区分。

Result: 未观察到相对标准模型预期的显著超出；在c_t=1、θ=0基准情形下，质量低于1150 GeV的Z'玻色子被95%置信度排除，预期排除限为1250 GeV。

Conclusion: 结果对top-philic Z'模型给出严格限制，证实该类信号在高亮度LHC数据中未显现。

Abstract: A search for a new top-philic vector boson ($Z'$) produced in association with a top-quark or a pair of top quarks, and decaying into a top quark pair is presented. The search uses $pp$ collision data collected by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider at centre-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} =$ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV, corresponding to integrated luminosities of 140 fb$^{-1}$ and 56 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. Events containing exactly two same-sign leptons or at least three leptons (electrons or muons) are selected. A transformer-based classifier parameterised as a function of the $Z'$ mass is used to optimise the signal-to-background discrimination. The results are interpreted in a simplified model with a colour-singlet vector boson coupled to top quarks with coupling strength $c_t$ and chirality angle $θ$. No significant excess is observed above the expected Standard Model background. A $Z'$ boson with mass below 1150 GeV is excluded at 95% confidence level for a benchmark scenario with $c_t = 1$ and $θ= 0$, with an expected limit of 1250 GeV.

</details>
